# Why It Sucks to Be a Sports Fan (in Byzantine Constantinople) — Image + Voiceover Prompts

298 beats · ~3–5 s each · Visual Style Profile locked · Characters locked to the Yesteryears character sheets

**Voiceover:** every beat has a direction in [emotion] tag form plus pace, emphasis and pause notes. The tags work directly in ElevenLabs v3 and similar tools; for other voices, read them as acting notes. Keep the base voice calm, deadpan and close-mic, and let the tags shift it.

**Headwear rule:** headwear fits the character's class and the scene. Everyone in this episode is bareheaded except the Emperor and Empress, whose diadems are royal regalia.

**Character lock**

| Story role | Sheet |
|---|---|
| You, age 4 / 6 / 7 | Boy variant, bareheaded |
| You, 16 / 18 / partisan | Mascot (partisan haircut, beard and green puffed sleeves from 18) |
| Your mother | Adult woman variant |
| Father, Leon, Leon's father, Porphyrius, Justinian, Theodora, John, Hypatius, Narses, Belisarius, Procopius, monks | Not on a sheet (fixed descriptions) |
